Module
SatThresholdDefinitions
:= Struct
     Definition sat_prob :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat) (_ : nat),
       reals.Real.sort R.
   End
SatThresholdDefinitions.sat_prob =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (n k m : nat) =>
@SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_mean R n k m
  (fun t : tuple.tuple_of m (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k) =>
   @nmodule.Algebra.natmul
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
     (ssrnat.nat_of_bool (@SatVarianceDefinitions.prefix_sat n k m t m)))
     : forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat) (_ : nat),
       reals.Real.sort R

Arguments SatThresholdDefinitions.sat_prob R (n k m)%_nat_scope

SatThresholdStatement
:= Struct Definition sat_threshold_statement : Prop. End
SatThresholdStatement.sat_threshold_statement =
forall (R : reals.Real.type) (k : nat)
  (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S (S (S O))) k)),
@ex (reals.Real.sort R)
  (fun alpha : reals.Real.sort R =>
   and
     (is_true
        (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
           (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
           (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
              (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R)))
           alpha))
     (and
        (forall (c : reals.Real.sort R)
           (_ : is_true
                  (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
                     (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule
                           R))
                     (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
                        (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule
                              R)))
                     c))
           (_ : is_true
                  (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R) c
                     alpha)),
         @filter.cvg_to (reals.Real.sort R)
           (@filter.nbhs (reals.Real.sort R)
              (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                 (reals.Real.sort R))
              (@filter.fmap nat (reals.Real.sort R)
                 (fun n : nat =>
                  SatThresholdDefinitions.sat_prob R n k
                    (@archimedean.Num.truncn
                       (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_ArchiNumDomain
                          R)
                       (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R)
                          c
                          (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                   R))
                             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                   R))
                             n))))
                 (@filter.nbhs nat
                    (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                       nat)
                    filter.eventually)))
           (@filter.nbhs (reals.Real.sort R)
              (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_Filtered
                 R)
              (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
               :
               reals.Real.sort R)))
        (forall (c : reals.Real.sort R)
           (_ : is_true
                  (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R)
                     alpha c)),
         @filter.cvg_to (reals.Real.sort R)
           (@filter.nbhs (reals.Real.sort R)
              (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                 (reals.Real.sort R))
              (@filter.fmap nat (reals.Real.sort R)
                 (fun n : nat =>
                  SatThresholdDefinitions.sat_prob R n k
                    (@archimedean.Num.truncn
                       (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_ArchiNumDomain
                          R)
                       (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R)
                          c
                          (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                   R))
                             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                   R))
                             n))))
                 (@filter.nbhs nat
                    (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                       nat)
                    filter.eventually)))
           (@filter.nbhs (reals.Real.sort R)
              (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_Filtered
                 R)
              (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma R)
               :
               reals.Real.sort R)))))
     : Prop

MinSuperadditiveStatement
:= Struct Definition min_superadditive_statement : Prop. End
MinSuperadditiveStatement.min_superadditive_statement =
forall (R : reals.Real.type) (u : forall _ : nat, reals.Real.sort R)
  (A beta : reals.Real.sort R) (N : nat)
  (_ : is_true
         (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
            (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
               (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R)))
            A))
  (_ : is_true
         (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
            (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
               (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R)))
            beta))
  (_ : forall (r s : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq N r))
         (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq N s))
         (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq r (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S O))) s)))
         (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq s (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S O))) r))),
       is_true
         (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
            (@nmodule.Algebra.add
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R)
               (@preorder.Order.min orderedzmod.ring_display
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R) 
                  (u r) (u s))
               (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule R)
                  (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
                     A
                     (@exp.powR R
                        (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 R))
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 R))
                           (ssrnat.minn r s))
                        (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
                           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
                              R)
                           beta)))))
            (u (ssrnat.addn r s))))
  (_ : forall (s : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq N s)),
       is_true
         (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
            (@nmodule.Algebra.add
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R)
               (u s)
               (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule R)
                  (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
                     A
                     (@exp.powR R
                        (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 R))
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 R))
                           s)
                        (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
                           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
                              R)
                           beta)))))
            (u (S s)))),
let E :
  rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
    (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R) :=
  @rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
    (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
    (@exp.powR R
       (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
             (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
             (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
          (S (S (S O))))
       beta)
    (@divalg.GRing.inv (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
       (@nmodule.Algebra.add
          (nmodule.Algebra.BaseAddUMagma.Exports.Algebra_BaseAddUMagma__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)))
          (@exp.powR R
             (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                   (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R))
                (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                   (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                      (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                         (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
                   (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                      (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                         (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
                   (S (S (S O))))
                (@divalg.GRing.inv
                   (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
                   (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                         (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                            (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                               R)))
                      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                         (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                            (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                               R)))
                      (S (S O)))))
             beta)
          (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.GRing_PzRing__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))
             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
                   (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))))))
  in
and
  (forall (s n : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq N s))
     (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S (S O)) s))
     (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (ssrnat.muln s s) n)),
   is_true
     (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
        (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddMagma_and_Order_Preorder
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
        (@nmodule.Algebra.add
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R) 
           (u s)
           (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule R)
              (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
                 (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                    (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R) A
                    (@nmodule.Algebra.add
                       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R))
                       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R))
                       E))
                 (@exp.powR R
                    (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R))
                       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                             R))
                       s)
                    (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
                       (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
                          R)
                       beta)))))
        (u n)))
  (forall
     _ : @ex (reals.Real.sort R)
           (fun M : reals.Real.sort R =>
            forall n : nat,
            is_true
              (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
                 (orderedzmod.Num.POrderZmodule.Exports.Num_POrderZmodule__to__Order_Preorder
                    (@orderedzmod.Num.POrderZmodule.Pack 
                       (reals.Real.sort R)
                       (@orderedzmod.Num.POrderZmodule.Class
                          (reals.Real.sort R)
                          (@reals.Real.choice_hasChoice_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.eqtype_hasDecEq_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Order_isDuallyPreorder_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Order_Preorder_isDuallyPOrder_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasOpp_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasZero_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasAdd_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseZmoduleNmodule_isZmodule_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_isAddUMagma_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseAddMagma_isAddMagma_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_AddMagma_isAddSemigroup_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R)))))
                 (@numdomain.Num.norm
                    (@orderedzmod.Num.POrderZmodule.Pack 
                       (reals.Real.sort R)
                       (@orderedzmod.Num.POrderZmodule.Class
                          (reals.Real.sort R)
                          (@reals.Real.choice_hasChoice_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.eqtype_hasDecEq_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Order_isDuallyPreorder_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Order_Preorder_isDuallyPOrder_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasOpp_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasZero_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_hasAdd_mixin _
                             (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseZmoduleNmodule_isZmodule_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_isAddUMagma_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_BaseAddMagma_isAddMagma_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))
                          (@reals.Real.Algebra_AddMagma_isAddSemigroup_mixin
                             _ (reals.Real.class R))))
                    (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_SemiNormedZmodule
                       R)
                    (u n))
                 M)),
   and
     (@filter.cvg_to (reals.Real.sort R)
        (@filter.nbhs (reals.Real.sort R)
           (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
              (reals.Real.sort R))
           (@filter.fmap nat (reals.Real.sort R) u
              (@filter.nbhs nat
                 (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                    nat)
                 filter.eventually)))
        (@filter.nbhs
           (filter.PointedNbhs.sort
              (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_PointedNbhs
                 R))
           (@filter.PointedFiltered.Exports.filter_PointedFiltered__to__filter_Filtered

                 (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_PointedNbhs
                    R))
              (filter.PointedNbhs.Exports.filter_PointedNbhs__to__filter_PointedFiltered
                 (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_PointedNbhs
                    R)))
           (@filter.lim
              (num_topology.numFieldTopology.Real_sort__canonical__filter_PointedNbhs
                 R)
              (@filter.fmap nat (reals.Real.sort R) u
                 (@filter.nbhs nat
                    (filter.classical_sets_set_system__canonical__filter_Filtered
                       nat)
                    filter.eventually)))))
     (forall (s : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq N s))
        (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S (S O)) s)),

           (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddMagma_and_Order_Preorder

PsdNormalizationStatement
:= Struct Definition psd_normalization_statement : Prop. End
PsdNormalizationStatement.psd_normalization_statement =
forall (R : reals.Real.type) (I J : fintype.Finite.type) 
  (r : nat)
  (F : forall _ : fintype.Finite.sort I,
       matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) r r)
  (G : forall _ : fintype.Finite.sort J,
       matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) r r)
  (m : reals.Real.sort R)
  (_ : is_true
         (ssrnat.leq (S O)
            (@fintype.card.body J
               (@ssrbool.mem
                  (eqtype.Equality.sort
                     (fintype.Finite.Exports.fintype_Finite__to__eqtype_Equality
                        J))
                  (ssrbool.predPredType
                     (eqtype.Equality.sort
                        (fintype.Finite.Exports.fintype_Finite__to__eqtype_Equality
                           J)))
                  (@ssrbool.PredOfSimpl.coerce
                     (eqtype.Equality.sort
                        (fintype.Finite.Exports.fintype_Finite__to__eqtype_Equality
                           J))
                     (ssrbool.pred_of_argType
                        (eqtype.Equality.sort
                           (fintype.Finite.Exports.fintype_Finite__to__eqtype_Equality
                              J))))))))
  (_ : forall i : fintype.Finite.sort I, @psd_part.psdmx R r (F i))
  (_ : forall j : fintype.Finite.sort J, @psd_part.psdmx R r (G j))
  (_ : forall (i : fintype.Finite.sort I) (j : fintype.Finite.sort J),
       is_true
         (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
            (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
            (@matrix.mxtrace
               (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_Nmodule
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))

                                   PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set.u0}
                                   fintype.Finite.type ssrbool.predArgType
                                   (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite
                                      n)
                                   (fintype.ordinal n)))
                             (fun
                                U0 : finset.set_of
                                       (@reverse_coercion@{fintype.Finite.type.u0
                                          PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set.u0}
                                          fintype.Finite.type
                                          ssrbool.predArgType
                                          (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite
                                             n)
                                          (fintype.ordinal n)) =>
                              ssrnat.odd
                                (@fintype.card.body
                                   (@reverse_coercion@{fintype.Finite.type.u0
                                      PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set.u0}
                                      fintype.Finite.type ssrbool.predArgType
                                      (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite
                                         n)
                                      (fintype.ordinal n))

                                      (fintype.Finite.sort

                 U)))))
     : forall [n : nat] (_ : PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set n)
         (_ : PsdRankDefinitions.perfect_matching n),
       nat

PsdRankDefinitions.shifted_matching_mx =
fun (n : nat) (R : reals.Real.type) (rho : reals.Real.sort R)
  (U : PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set n)
  (M : PsdRankDefinitions.perfect_matching n) =>
@nmodule.Algebra.add
  (nmodule.Algebra.BaseAddUMagma.Exports.Algebra_BaseAddUMagma__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))))
  (@nmodule.Algebra.add
     (nmodule.Algebra.BaseAddUMagma.Exports.Algebra_BaseAddUMagma__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))))
     (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
        (@PsdRankDefinitions.crossing n U M))
     (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.GRing_PzRing__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))

           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))))
  rho
     : forall [n : nat] [R : reals.Real.type] (_ : reals.Real.sort R)
         (_ : PsdRankDefinitions.odd_set n)
         (_ : PsdRankDefinitions.perfect_matching n),
       reals.Real.sort R

binomial_bounds.central_bin_le
     : forall n : nat,
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq
            (ssrnat.muln (S (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S O))) n))
               (ssrnat.expn (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) n) n)
                  (S (S O))))
            (ssrnat.expn
               (S
                  (S
                     (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S O))))))))))))))))
               n))
binomial_bounds.central_bin_ge
     : forall (n : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S O) n)),
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq
            (ssrnat.expn
               (S
                  (S
                     (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S (S O))))))))))))))))
               n)
            (ssrnat.muln (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S (S O)))) n)
               (ssrnat.expn (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) n) n)
                  (S (S O)))))
binomial_bounds.half_bin_ge
     : forall h : nat,
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq (ssrnat.expn (S (S (S (S O)))) h)
            (ssrnat.muln (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) (S h))
               (ssrnat.expn (binomial.binomial h (ssrnat.half h)) (S (S O)))))
binomial_bounds.leq_bin_half
     : forall n k : nat,
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq (binomial.binomial n k)
            (binomial.binomial n (ssrnat.half n)))
binomial_bounds.bin_near_half
     : forall (h g T : nat)
         (_ : is_true
                (ssrnat.leq (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S (S (S (S O)))))) T) h))
         (_ : is_true
                (ssrnat.leq h (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) (ssrnat.addn g T))))
         (_ : is_true
                (ssrnat.leq (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) g)
                   (ssrnat.addn h (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) T)))),
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq (binomial.binomial h (ssrnat.half h))
            (ssrnat.muln (ssrnat.expn (S (S O)) (S T))
               (binomial.binomial h g)))
binomial_bounds.bin_sqr_central
     : forall (u j : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq j u)),
       @eq nat
         (ssrnat.muln (ssrnat.expn (binomial.binomial u j) (S (S O)))
            (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) u) u))
         (ssrnat.muln
            (ssrnat.muln (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) j) j)
               (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) (ssrnat.subn u j))
                  (ssrnat.subn u j)))
            (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) u)
               (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) j)))
binomial_bounds.bin_sqr_ratio
     : forall (u j : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq j u)),
       is_true
         (ssrnat.leq
            (ssrnat.muln
               (ssrnat.muln
                  (ssrnat.expn (binomial.binomial u j) (S (S (S (S O)))))
                  (S j))
               (S (ssrnat.subn u j)))
            (ssrnat.muln (ssrnat.muln (S (S (S (S O)))) (S u))
               (ssrnat.expn
                  (binomial.binomial (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) u)
                     (ssrnat.muln (S (S O)) j))
                  (S (S O)))))
